# Skills · Q · part 16

867 entries, query to question-bank. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **query**: Query the semantic dependency graph stored in .code-atlas/graph-schema.json. Accepts a JSON query object with one of four operations — dependencies_of, dependents_of, filter, transitive_dependents — and returns a filtered subgraph (matched nodes + relevant edges) with a plain-text summary. Use for impact analysis, architecture exploration, and risk identification. Triggers on: query code atlas, find dependencies, who imports, blast radius, impact analysis, find critical modules, query graph. ([MisterVitoPro/code-atlas](https://github.com/MisterVitoPro/code-atlas/tree/HEAD/skills/query))
- **query**: Use when the user invokes /llm-wiki:query or asks a question to be answered from an OKF bundle. Also covers finding authoritative sources when the bundle cannot answer. ([mlarkin00/plugins](https://github.com/mlarkin00/plugins/tree/HEAD/llm-wiki/skills/query))
